(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > wakeword.py << 'EOF'
 config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX

# Locate the bundled pretrained model file inside the install> import sounddevice as sd
 with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dt> import openwakeword
e=CHU> from openwakeword.model import Model
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
> # Locate the bundled pretrained model file inside the installed package
> _pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
> _MODEL_PATH = f"{_pkg_dir}/resources/models/hey_jarvis_v0.1.onnx"
>
> owwModel = Model(wakeword_model_paths=[_MODEL_PATH])
>
>
> def listen_for_wakeword():
>     """Blocks until the wake word is heard, then returns."""
>     CHUNK = 1280
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         print("Listening for wake word...")
>         while True:
>             audio, _ = stream.read(CHUNK)
>             audio = audio.flatten()
>             prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>             for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                 if score > 0.5:
>                     print(f"Wake word detected: {mdl} ({score:.2f})")
>                     return
> EOF

(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > brain.py << 'EOF'
> import speech_recognition as sr
> from google import genai
> from google.genai import types
> from config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
> from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
>
> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since your
> reply will be spoken out loud. Use the provided tools whenever the user asks about the
> time or weather — never guess live data."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Free speech-to-text using Google's Web Speech API (no key needed)."""
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>     try:
>         # try English first, then Hindi, then Urdu
>         for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>             try:
>                 return recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang)
>             except sr.UnknownValueError:
>                 continue
>         return ""
>     except sr.RequestError as e:
>         return f"[STT error: {e}]"
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Transcribes the recorded command, then asks Gemini for a short spoken reply."""
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > brain.py << 'EOF'
> import speech_recognition as sr
> from google import genai
> from google.genai import types
> from config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
> from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
>
> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud. Use the provided tools whenever the user asks about the
> time or weather — never guess live data."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Free speech-to-text using Google's Web Speech API (no key needed)."""
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>     try:
>         for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>             try:
>                 return recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang)
>             except sr.UnknownValueError:
>                 continue
>         return ""
>     except sr.RequestError as e:
>         return f"[STT error: {e}]"
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Transcribes the recorded command, then asks Gemini for a short spoken reply."""
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF
(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > listener.py << 'EOF'
> import wave
> import sounddevice as sd
> import webrtcvad
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
>
> def record_command(out_path: str = "command.wav", max_seconds: int = 8) -> str:
>     """Records audio after the wake word until ~0.5s of silence is detected."""
>     SAMPLE_RATE = 16000
>     FRAME_MS = 30
>     FRAME_SIZE = int(SAMPLE_RATE * FRAME_MS / 1000)
>     vad = webrtcvad.Vad(2)
>
>     frames = []
>     silence_run = 0
>     triggered = False
>     max_frames = int(max_seconds * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=SAMPLE_RATE, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=FRAME_SIZE, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         print("Listening for your command...")
>         for _ in range(max_frames):
>             audio, _ = stream.read(FRAME_SIZE)
>             pcm = audio.tobytes()
>             is_speech = vad.is_speech(pcm, SAMPLE_RATE)
>             frames.append(pcm)
>             if is_speech:
>                 triggered = True
>                 silence_run = 0
>             elif triggered:
>                 silence_run += 1
>                 if silence_run > int(500 / FRAME_MS):  # ~0.5s of silence
>                     break
>
>     with wave.open(out_path, "wb") as wf:
>         wf.setnchannels(1)
>         wf.setsampwidth(2)
>         wf.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE)
>         wf.writeframes(b"".join(frames))
>
>     return out_path
> EOF

(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > brain.py << 'EOF'
rom config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
from tools import get_current_tim> import speech_recognition as sr
> from google import genai
> from google.genai import types
> from config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
> from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
>
> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud. Use the provided tools whenever the user asks about the
> time or weather — never guess live data."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Free speech-to-text using Google's Web Speech API, checking en/hi/ur
>     and picking whichever result has the highest confidence."""
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>
>     best_text = ""
>     best_conf = -1.0
>
>     for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>         try:
>             result = recognizer.recognize_google(
>                 audio, language=lang, show_all=True
>             )
>             if not result or "alternative" not in result:
>                 continue
>             top = result["alternative"][0]
>             text = top.get("transcript", "")
>             conf = top.get("confidence", 0.5)  # Google doesn't always give confidence
>             if text and conf > best_conf:
>                 best_text = text
>                 best_conf = conf
>         except (sr.UnknownValueError, sr.RequestError):
>             continue
>
>     return best_text
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Transcribes the recorded command, then asks Gemini for a short spoken reply."""
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

> from brain import ask_gemini_with_audio
> from tts import speak
>
>
> def main():
>     print("Assistant ready. Say the wake word to start.")
>     while True:
>         try:
>             listen_for_wakeword()
>
>             # Check if the user kept talking right after the wake word
>             already_talking = quick_speech_check()
>
>             if not already_talking:
>                 speak("Yes sir?", out_path="ack.mp3")
>
>             audio_path = record_command()
audio_p>             reply = ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path)
>             print("Assistant:", reply)
>             speak(reply)
>         except KeyboardInterrupt:
>             print("Stopping assistant.")
>             break
>         except Exception as e:
>             print("Error:", e)
>
>
> if __name__ == "__main__":
>     main()
> EOF

> def quick_speech_check(window_seconds: float = 1.2) -> bool:
>     """Right after the wake word, listens briefly to see if the user kept talking.
>     Returns True if speech was detected (so we skip the 'Yes sir?' and go straight
>     to recording), False if they went quiet (so we prompt them)."""
>     frames_needed = int(window_seconds * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=SAMPLE_RATE, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=FRAME_SIZE, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         for _ in range(frames_needed):
>             audio, _ = stream.read(FRAME_SIZE)
>             if vad.is_speech(audio.tobytes(), SAMPLE_RATE):
>                 return True
>     return False
>
>
> def record_command(out_path: str = "command.wav", max_seconds: int = 8) -> str:
>     """Records audio until ~0.5s of silence is detected."""

>     silence_run = 0
>     triggered = False
>     max_frames = int(max_seconds * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>

> # Common Roku channel/app IDs (add more as needed)
> APP_IDS = {
>     "youtube": "837",
>     "netflix": "12",
>     "prime video": "13",
>     "amazon prime": "13",
>     "disney plus": "291097",
>     "hulu": "2285",
>     "spotify": "22297",
> }
>
>
> def launch_app(app_name: str) -> str:
>     """Launches an app on the Roku by name."""
>     key = app_name.lower().strip()
>     app_id = APP_IDS.get(key)
>     if not app_id:
>         return f"I don't have '{app_name}' saved for your Roku yet."
>     resp = requests.post(f"{ROKU_URL}/launch/{app_id}", timeout=5)
>     if resp.status_code == 200:
>         return f"Opening {app_name} on your TV."
>     return f"Couldn't open {app_name} on the Roku."
>
>
> def play_on_youtube(query: str) -> str:
>     """Searches YouTube on the Roku and plays the first matching video."""
>     # Launch YouTube with a search query and auto-play the top result
>     params = {
>         "contentID": "",
>         "MediaType": "movie",
>         "search": query,
>     }
>     url = f"{ROKU_URL}/launch/837?{'&'.join(f'{k}={quote(str(v))}' for k, v in params.items())}"
>     resp = requests.post(url, timeout=5)
>     if resp.status_code == 200:
>         return f"Searching YouTube for {query} on your TV."
>     return f"Couldn't start YouTube search for {query}."
>
>
> def get_roku_apps() -> str:
>     """Lists installed apps on the Roku (for debugging what's available)."""
>     resp = requests.get(f"{ROKU_URL}/query/apps", timeout=5)
>     root = ET.fromstring(resp.text)
>     apps = [app.text for app in root.findall("app")]
>     return ", ".join(apps)
> EOF

        ydl_opts = {

            "default_search": "ytsearch1",

        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_op> import subprocess
> import yt_dlp

>
> def play_song(query: str) -> str:
>     """Searches YouTube for a song and streams just the audio through the speaker.
>     Nothing is saved to disk — mpv streams it directly."""
>     try:
>         ydl_opts = {
>             "format": "bestaudio/best",
>             "quiet": True,
>             "noplaylist": True,
>             "default_search": "ytsearch1",
>         }
>         with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
>             info = ydl.extract_info(query, download=False)
>             if "entries" in info:
>                 info = info["entries"][0]
>             stream_url = info["url"]
>             title = info.get("title", query)
>
>         # Play in the background so the assistant isn't frozen while it plays
>         subprocess.Popen(
>             ["mpv", "--no-terminal", "--really-quiet", stream_url],
>             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
>         )
>         return f"Playing {title}."
>     except Exception as e:
>         return f"Sorry, I couldn't play that song. ({e})"
> EOF

rom config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
from music import play_song
fro> import speech_recognition as sr
> from google import genai
> from google.genai import types

> from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
> from music import play_song
> from roku import launch_app, play_on_youtube
>
> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
T write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
(e.g. سلام)> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud.
>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - play_song when the user wants to hear music through the speaker right now (e.g. "play
>   [song name]", "put on some music").
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
> - play_on_youtube when the user wants to watch something specific on their TV/Roku screen
>   (e.g. "play [video/song] on the TV", "show me [x] on the screen")."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>
>     best_text = ""
>     best_conf = -1.0
>     for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>         try:
>             result = recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang, show_all=True)
>             if not result or "alternative" not in result:
>                 continue
>             top = result["alternative"][0]
>             text = top.get("transcript", "")
0.5)
  >             conf = top.get("confidence", 0.5)
>             if text and conf > best_conf:
>                 best_text = text
>                 best_conf = conf
>         except (sr.UnknownValueError, sr.RequestError):
>             continue
>     return best_text
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, play_song, launch_app, play_on_youtube],
F>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

>
> def launch_app(app_name: str) -> str:
>     """Launches an app on the Roku by name."""
>     key = app_name.lower().strip()
>     app_id = APP_IDS.get(key)
>     if not app_id:
>         return f"I don't have '{app_name}' saved for your Roku yet."
>     resp = requests.post(f"{ROKU_URL}/launch/{app_id}", timeout=5)
>     if resp.status_code == 200:
>         return f"Opening {app_name} on your TV."
>     return f"Couldn't open {app_name} on the Roku."
>
>
> def _find_video_id(query: str, channel: str = "", latest_from_channel: bool = False) -> tuple:
>     """Finds a specific YouTube video ID using yt-dlp search, optionally scoped to
>     a channel, or the latest upload from that channel."""
>     ydl_opts = {"quiet": True, "extract_flat": True, "noplaylist": True}
>
>     if latest_from_channel and channel:
>         # Fetch the channel's most recent upload
>         search_term = f"ytsearch1:{channel} official channel"
>         with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
>             chan_info = ydl.extract_info(search_term, download=False)
>             entries = chan_info.get("entries", [])
>             if not entries:
>                 return None, None
>             channel_url = entries[0].get("url") or entries[0].get("channel_url")
>             # Pull the channel's uploads, most recent first
>             uploads_url = f"{channel_url}/videos" if channel_url else None
>             if not uploads_url:
>                 return None, None
>             uploads = ydl.extract_info(uploads_url, download=False)
>             vids = uploads.get("entries", [])
>             if not vids:
>                 return None, None
>             top = vids[0]
>             return top.get("id"), top.get("title", query)
>
>     # Normal search, optionally including the channel name to bias results
>     full_query = f"{query} {channel}".strip() if channel else query
>     with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
>         info = ydl.extract_info(f"ytsearch1:{full_query}", download=False)
>         entries = info.get("entries", [])
>         if not entries:
>             return None, None
>         top = entries[0]
>         return top.get("id"), top.get("title", query)
>
>
> def play_on_youtube(query: str, channel: str = "", latest_from_channel: bool = False) -> str:
>     """Finds a specific YouTube video (optionally from a named channel, or that
>     channel's latest upload) and plays it directly on the Roku's screen.
>
>     query: what to search for (e.g. "Surah Rahman", can be empty if latest_from_channel)
>     channel: channel name to search within (e.g. "iRecite", "TBV")
>     latest_from_channel: True if the user wants that channel's newest video
>     """
>     video_id, title = _find_video_id(query, channel, latest_from_channel)
>     if not video_id:
>         return f"Couldn't find that video on YouTube."
>
>     resp = requests.post(
>         f"{ROKU_URL}/launch/837?contentID={video_id}&mediaType=video",
>         timeout=5,
>     )
>     if resp.status_code == 200:
>         return f"Playing {title} on your TV."
>     return f"Found {title} but couldn't start it on the Roku."
>
>
> def get_roku_apps() -> str:
>     resp = requests.get(f"{ROKU_URL}/query/apps", timeout=5)
>     root = ET.fromstring(resp.text)
>     apps = [app.text for app in root.findall("app")]
>     return ", ".join(apps)
> EOF

>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - play_song when the user wants to hear music through the SPEAKER only (e.g. "play some music").
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to play
>   (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
> - play_on_youtube when the user wants to watch something on their TV/Roku SCREEN. Pass:
>   - query: what they want to watch (leave empty if they just want the channel's latest video)
>   - channel: the channel name if they mentioned one (e.g. "iRecite", "TBV") - empty string if none
>   - latest_from_channel: true if they said things like "latest video", "newest upload" from a channel
>   Examples: "play Surah Rahman from iRecite on TV" -> query="Surah Rahman", channel="iRecite",
>   latest_from_channel=false. "play TBV's latest video on TV" -> query="", channel="TBV",
>   latest_from_channel=true."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>
>     best_text = ""
>     best_conf = -1.0
>     for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>         try:
>             result = recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang, show_all=True)
>             if not result or "alternative" not in result:
>                 continue
>             top = result["alternative"][0]
>             text = top.get("transcript", "")
)
     >             conf = top.get("confidence", 0.5)
>             if text and conf > best_conf:
>                 best_text = text
>                 best_conf = conf
>         except (sr.UnknownValueError, sr.RequestError):
>             continue
>     return best_text
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:

>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
   conte>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, play_song, launch_app, play_on_youtube],
>         ),
>     )

(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > brain.py << 'EOF'
import speech_recognition as sr
from google import genai
from google.genai import types
from config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
from music import play_song
from roku import launch_app, play_on_youtube

client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)

SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
(e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
your reply will be spoken out loud.

Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
- get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
- play_song when the user wants to hear music through the SPEAKER only (e.g. "play some music").
- launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to play
  (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
- play_on_youtube when the user wants to watch something on their TV/Roku SCREEN. Pass:
  - query: what they want to watch (leave empty if they just want the channel's latest video)
  - channel: the channel name if they mentioned one (e.g. "iRecite", "TBV") - empty string if none
  - latest_from_channel: true if they said things like "latest video", "newest upload" from a channel
  Examples: "play Surah Rahman from iRecite on TV" -> query="Surah Rahman", channel="iRecite",
  latest_from_channel=false. "play TBV's latest video on TV" -> query="", channel="TBV",
  latest_from_channel=true."""

recognizer = sr.Recognizer()


def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
    with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
        audio = recognizer.record(source)

    best_text = ""
    best_conf = -1.0
    for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
        try:
            result = recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang, show_all=True)
            if not result or "alternative" not in result:
                continue

>         return f"Opening {app_name} on your TV."
>     return f"Couldn't open {app_name} on the Roku."
>
>
> def play_video_id(video_id: str, title: str = "") -> str:
>     resp = requests.post(
>         f"{ROKU_URL}/launch/837?contentID={video_id}&mediaType=video",
>         timeout=5,
>     )
>     if resp.status_code == 200:
>         return f"Playing {title} on your TV." if title else "Playing that on your TV."
>     return "Found it but couldn't start it on the Roku."
>
>
> def get_roku_apps() -> str:
>     resp = requests.get(f"{ROKU_URL}/query/apps", timeout=5)
>     root = ET.fromstring(resp.text)
>     apps = [app.text for app in root.findall("app")]
>     return ", ".join(apps)
> EOF
(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > media_tool.py << 'EOF'
> import state
> from media import search_candidates, format_choices_for_speech
> from music import play_by_id
> from roku import play_video_id
]
    state.pending_selection = None
    if target == "tv":
        return play_video_id(c["id"], c["title"])
    return play_by_id(c["id"], c["title>
>
> def search_and_play(query: str = "", channel: str = "", latest_from_channel: bool = False, target: str = "speaker") -> str:
>     candidates = search_candidates(query, channel, latest_from_channel)
>     if not candidates:
>         return "I couldn't find anything matching that."
>
>     if len(candidates) == 1:
>         c = candidates[0]
>         if target == "tv":
>             return play_video_id(c["id"], c["title"])
>         return play_by_id(c["id"], c["title"])
>
>     state.pending_selection = {"candidates": candidates, "target": target}
>     listing = format_choices_for_speech(candidates)
>     return f"I found a few matches. {listing} Which one?"
>
>
> def resolve_selection(choice_num: int):
>     sel = state.pending_selection
>     if not sel:
>         return None
>     candidates = sel["candidates"]
>     target = sel["target"]
>     if choice_num < 1 or choice_num > len(candidates):
>         return "That number isn't one of the options."
>     c = candidates[choice_num - 1]
>     state.pending_selection = None
>     if target == "tv":
>         return play_video_id(c["id"], c["title"])
>     return play_by_id(c["id"], c["title"])
> EOF

> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud.
>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to
>   play (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
> - search_and_play for ANY request to play a song, video, surah, or anything else, whether
>   through the speaker or on the TV/Roku screen. Pass:
>   - query: what they want (leave empty if they only named a channel + "latest video")
>   - channel: the channel name if mentioned, empty string if none
>   - latest_from_channel: true if they asked for that channel's newest/latest upload
>   - target: "speaker" if they want to just hear it, "tv" if they said TV/screen/Roku
>   If search_and_play returns a numbered list asking "which one?", just read that exact
>   reply back to the user as-is - don't add anything else, they'll answer with a number next."""
>
>
> def ask_gemini(user_text: str) -> str:
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, launch_app, search_and_play],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

 Model
from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX

_pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
_MODE> import time
> import sounddevice as sd
> import openwakeword
> from openwakeword.model import Model
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
> _pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
> _MODEL_PATH = f"{_pkg_dir}/resources/models/hey_jarvis_v0.1.onnx"
>
> owwModel = Model(wakeword_model_paths=[_MODEL_PATH])
>
>
> def listen_for_wakeword():
>     CHUNK = 1280
>     while True:
>         try:
>             with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                                  blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>                 print("Listening for wake word...")
>                 while True:
>                     audio, overflowed = stream.read(CHUNK)
>                     if overflowed:
>                         continue
>                     audio = audio.flatten()
>                     prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>                     for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                         if score > 0.5:
>                             print(f"Wake word detected: {mdl} ({score:.2f})")
>                             return
>         except Exception as e:
>             print(f"Mic stream error ({e}) - reconnecting in 2s...")
>             time.sleep(2)
>             continue
> EOF

>
> def _send_key(key: str) -> bool:
>     resp = requests.post(f"{ROKU_URL}/keypress/{key}", timeout=5)
>     return resp.status_code == 200
>
>
> def playback_control(action: str) -> str:
>     action = action.lower().strip()
>     if action in ("pause", "play"):
>         if _send_key("Play"):
>             return "Paused." if action == "pause" else "Resumed."
>         return "Couldn't control playback."
>     if action == "rewind":
>         if _send_key("Rev"):
>             return "Rewinding."
>         return "Couldn't rewind."
>     if action == "forward":
>         if _send_key("Fwd"):
>             return "Fast-forwarding."
>         return "Couldn't fast-forward."
>     return "I don't know that playback action."
>
>
> def tv_power(state_action: str) -> str:
>     key = "PowerOn" if state_action.lower().strip() == "on" else "PowerOff"
>     if _send_key(key):
>         return "Turning the TV on." if key == "PowerOn" else "Turning the TV off."
>     return "Couldn't control TV power."
>
>
> def get_roku_apps() -> str:
>     resp = requests.get(f"{ROKU_URL}/query/apps", timeout=5)
>     root = ET.fromstring(resp.text)
>     apps = [app.text for app in root.findall("app")]
>     return ", ".join(apps)
> EOF

def pick_voice(text: str) -> str:

> def pick_voice(text: str) -> str:
>     if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", text):
>         return TTS_VOICE_HI
>     if re.search(r"[\u0600-\u06FF]", text):
>         return TTS_VOICE_UR
>     return TTS_VOICE_EN
>
>
> async def _synthesize(text: str, voice: str, out_path: str):
>     communicate = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice)
>     await communicate.save(out_path)
>
>
> def speak(text: str, out_path: str = "reply.mp3", interrupt_check=None):
>     voice = pick_voice(text)
>     asyncio.run(_synthesize(text, voice, out_path))
>
>     proc = subprocess.Popen(
>         ["mpv", "--no-terminal", "--really-quiet", out_path],
>         stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
>     )
>
>     if interrupt_check is None:
>         proc.wait()
>         return True
>
>     while proc.poll() is None:
>         if interrupt_check():
>             proc.terminate()
>             proc.wait()
>             return False
>     return True

>                             self._triggered = True
>                             return
>         except Exception:
>             return

> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud.
>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to
>   play (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV", "open my app").
> - search_and_play for ANY request to play a song, video, surah, or anything else, whether
>   through the speaker or on the TV/Roku screen. Pass:
>   - query: what they want (leave empty if they only named a channel + "latest video")
>   - channel: the channel name if mentioned, empty string if none
>   - latest_from_channel: true if they asked for that channel's newest/latest upload
>   - target: "speaker" if they want to just hear it, "tv" if they said TV/screen/Roku
>   If search_and_play returns a numbered list asking "which one?", just read that exact
>   reply back to the user as-is - don't add anything else, they'll answer with a number next.
> - playback_control when the user wants to pause, resume, rewind, or fast-forward whatever
>   is currently playing on the TV/Roku. action must be exactly "pause", "play", "rewind",
>   or "forward".
> - tv_power when the user wants to turn the TV on or off. state_action must be exactly
>   "on" or "off". Note: only power works reliably on this TV, not volume - if asked about
>   volume, apologize briefly and explain that isn't controllable on this TV."""
>
>
> def ask_gemini(user_text: str) -> str:
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, launch_app, search_and_play,
>                    playback_control, tv_power],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text

> class WakeWordWatcher:
>     def __init__(self):
>         self._triggered = False
>         self._stop_flag = False
>         self._thread = None
>
>     def start(self):
>         self._triggered = False
>         self._stop_flag = False
>         self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
>         self._thread.start()
>
>     def _run(self):
>         CHUNK = 1280
>         try:
>             with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                                  blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>                 while not self._stop_flag:
>                     audio, overflowed = stream.read(CHUNK)
>                     if overflowed:
>                         continue
>                     audio = audio.flatten()
>                     with _model_lock:
>                         prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>                     for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                         if score > 0.5:

 >                             self._triggered = True
>                             return
>         except Exception:
>             return
>
>     def triggered(self) -> bool:
>         return self._triggered
>
>     def stop(self):
>         self._stop_flag = True
>         if self._thread:
>             self._thread.join(timeout=1)

> def record_command(out_path: str = "command.wav", max_seconds: int = 8,
>                     prompt_after: float = 1.2) -> str:
>     frames = []
>     silence_run = 0
>     triggered = False
>     max_frames = int(max_seconds * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>     prompt_frame_limit = int(prompt_after * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>     prompted = False
>
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=SAMPLE_RATE, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=FRAME_SIZE, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         print("Listening...")
>         for i in range(max_frames):
>             audio, _ = stream.read(FRAME_SIZE)
>             pcm = audio.tobytes()
>             is_speech = vad.is_speech(pcm, SAMPLE_RATE)
>
>             if is_speech:
>                 triggered = True
>                 silence_run = 0
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>             elif triggered:
>                 silence_run += 1
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>                 if silence_run > int(500 / FRAME_MS):
>                     break
>             else:
>                 if i == prompt_frame_limit and not prompted:
>                     prompted = True
>                     play_prompt_sound()
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>                 if len(frames) > 10:
>                     frames.pop(0)
>
>     with wave.open(out_path, "wb") as wf:
>         wf.setnchannels(1)
>         wf.setsampwidth(2)
>         wf.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE)
>         wf.writeframes(b"".join(frames))
>
>     play_thinking_sound()
>     return out_path
>

     > import subprocess

>
>
> def pick_voice(text: str) -> str:
>     if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", text):
>         return TTS_VOICE_HI
>     if re.search(r"[\u0600-\u06FF]", text):
>         return TTS_VOICE_UR
>     return TTS_VOICE_EN
>
>
> async def _synthesize(text: str, voice: str, out_path: str):
>     communicate = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice)
>     await communicate.save(out_path)
>
>
> def speak(text: str, out_path: str = "reply.mp3", interrupt_check=None):
>     voice = pick_voice(text)
>     asyncio.run(_synthesize(text, voice, out_path))
>
>     proc = subprocess.Popen(
>         ["mpv", "--no-terminal", "--really-quiet", out_path],
>         stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
>     )
>
>     if interrupt_check is None:
>         proc.wait()
>         return True
>
>     while proc.poll() is None:
>         if interrupt_check():
>             proc.terminate()
>             proc.wait()
>             return False
>     return True
> PYEOF

SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
(e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. K> import speech_recognition as sr
> from google import genai
> from google.genai import types
> from config import GEMINI_API_KEY, ASSISTANT_NAME
> from tools import get_current_time, get_weather
>
> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud. Use the provided tools whenever the user asks about the
> time or weather — never guess live data."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Free speech-to-text using Google's Web Speech API, checking en/hi/ur
>     and picking whichever result has the highest confidence."""
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>
>     best_text = ""
>     best_conf = -1.0
>
>     for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>         try:
>             result = recognizer.recognize_google(
>                 audio, language=lang, show_all=True
>             )
>             if not result or "alternative" not in result:
>                 continue
>             top = result["alternative"][0]
>             text = top.get("transcript", "")
>             conf = top.get("confidence", 0.5)
>             if text and conf > best_conf:
>                 best_text = text
>                 best_conf = conf
>         except (sr.UnknownValueError, sr.RequestError):
>             continue
>
>     return best_text
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Transcribes the recorded command, then asks Gemini for a short spoken reply."""
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

import asyncio
import re
import sub(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > tts.py << 'EOF'
VOICE_UR


def pick_voice(text: str) -> str:
    if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", text):
        return TTS_VOICE_HI
    if re.search(r"[\u0600-\u06FF]", text):
        return TTS_VOICE_UR
    return TTS_VOICE_EN


async def _speak(text: str, voice: str, out_path: str):
    communicate = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice)
    await communicate.save(out_path)

> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)
>
> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud. Use the provided tools whenever the user asks about the
> time or weather — never guess live data."""
>
> recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
>
>
> def transcribe_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Free speech-to-text using Google's Web Speech API, checking en/hi/ur
>     and picking whichever result has the highest confidence."""
>     with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
>         audio = recognizer.record(source)
>
>     best_text = ""
>     best_conf = -1.0
>
>     for lang in ["en-US", "hi-IN", "ur-PK"]:
>         try:
>             result = recognizer.recognize_google(
>                 audio, language=lang, show_all=True
>             )
>             if not result or "alternative" not in result:
>                 continue
>             top = result["alternative"][0]
>             text = top.get("transcript", "")
>             conf = top.get("confidence", 0.5)
>             if text and conf > best_conf:
>                 best_text = text
>                 best_conf = conf
>         except (sr.UnknownValueError, sr.RequestError):
>             continue
>
>     return best_text
>
>
> def ask_gemini_with_audio(audio_path: str) -> str:
>     """Transcribes the recorded command, then asks Gemini for a short spoken reply."""
>     user_text = transcribe_audio(audio_path)
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

> owwModel = Model(wakeword_model_paths=[_MODEL_PATH])
>
>
> def listen_for_wakeword():
>     """Blocks until the wake word is heard, then returns."""
>     CHUNK = 1280
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         print("Listening for wake word...")
>         while True:
>             audio, _ = stream.read(CHUNK)
>             audio = audio.flatten()
>             prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>             for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                 if score > 0.5:
>                     print(f"Wake word detected: {mdl} ({score:.2f})")
>                     return

(pi-assistant-env) hacker@hacker:~/pi-assistant $ cat > listener.py << 'EOF'
> import wave
> import subprocess
> import sounddevice as sd
> import webrtcvad
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
> SAMPLE_RATE = 16000
> FRAME_MS = 30
> FRAME_SIZE = int(SAMPLE_RATE * FRAME_MS / 1000)
> vad = webrtcvad.Vad(2)
>
>
> def record_command(out_path: str = "command.wav", max_seconds: int = 8,
>                     prompt_after: float = 1.2) -> str:
>     """Records the user's command. If they start talking within `prompt_after`
>     seconds, keeps recording seamlessly. If they're silent that long, says
>     'Yes sir?' then keeps listening for the real question. Stops after ~0.5s
>     of trailing silence."""
>     frames = []
>     silence_run = 0
>     triggered = False
>     max_frames = int(max_seconds * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>     prompt_frame_limit = int(prompt_after * 1000 / FRAME_MS)
>     prompted = False
>
>     with sd.InputStream(samplerate=SAMPLE_RATE, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                          blocksize=FRAME_SIZE, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>         print("Listening...")
>         for i in range(max_frames):
>             audio, _ = stream.read(FRAME_SIZE)
>             pcm = audio.tobytes()
>             is_speech = vad.is_speech(pcm, SAMPLE_RATE)
>
>             if is_speech:
>                 triggered = True
>                 silence_run = 0
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>             elif triggered:
>                 silence_run += 1
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>                 if silence_run > int(500 / FRAME_MS):
>                     break
>             else:
>                 if i == prompt_frame_limit and not prompted:
>                     prompted = True
>                     play_prompt_sound()
>                 if i > max_frames:
>                     break
>                 frames.append(pcm)
>                 if len(frames) > 10:
>                     frames.pop(0)
>
>     with wave.open(out_path, "wb") as wf:
>         wf.setnchannels(1)
>         wf.setsampwidth(2)
>         wf.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE)
>         wf.writeframes(b"".join(frames))
>
>     play_thinking_sound()
):
  >     return out_path
>
>
> def play_prompt_sound():
>     """Plays 'Yes sir?' when the user paused after the wake word."""
>     subprocess.run(
>         ["mpv", "--no-terminal", "--really-quiet", "ack.mp3"],
>         stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
>     )
>
>
> def play_thinking_sound():
>     """Plays a short chime while Gemini/TTS are working, non-blocking."""
>     subprocess.Popen(
>         ["mpv", "--no-terminal", "--really-quiet", "thinking.mp3"],
>         stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
>     )
> EOF

hacker@hacker:~ $ cat > selection.py << 'EOF'
> import re
>
> _WORD_NUMBERS = {
>     "one": 1, "first": 1,
>     "two": 2, "second": 2,
>     "three": 3, "third": 3,
>     "four": 4, "fourth": 4,
>     "five": 5, "fifth": 5,
>     "six": 6, "sixth": 6,
>     "seven": 7, "seventh": 7,
>     "eight": 8, "eighth": 8,
>     "nine": 9, "ninth": 9,
>     "ten": 10, "tenth": 10,
> }
>
>
> def parse_selection(text: str, max_choice: int):
>     text_low = text.lower()
>     m = re.search(r"\b(\d{1,2})\b", text_low)
>     if m:
>         n = int(m.group(1))
>         if 1 <= n <= max_choice:
>             return n
>     for word, n in _WORD_NUMBERS.items():
>         if re.search(rf"\b{word}\b", text_low) and n <= max_choice:
>             return n
>     return None
> EOF

>
> def search_and_play(query: str = "", channel: str = "", latest_from_channel: bool = False, target: str = "speaker") -> str:
>     candidates = search_candidates(query, channel, latest_from_channel)
>     if not candidates:
>         return "I couldn't find anything matching that."
>
>     if len(candidates) == 1:
>         c = candidates[0]

>
>     state.pending_selection = {"candidates": candidates, "target": target}
>     listing = format_choices_for_speech(candidates)
>     return f"I found a few matches. {listing} Which one?"
>
>
> def resolve_selection(choice_num: int):
>     sel = state.pending_selection
>     if not sel:
>         return None
>     candidates = sel["candidates"]
>     target = sel["target"]
>     if choice_num < 1 or choice_num > len(candidates):
>         return "That number isn't one of the options."
>     c = candidates[choice_num - 1]
>     state.pending_selection = None
>     if target == "tv":
>         return play_video_id(c["id"], c["title"])
>     return play_by_id(c["id"], c["title"])

> client = genai.Client(api_key=GEMINI_API_KEY)

> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud.
>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to
>   play (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
> - search_and_play for ANY request to play a song, video, surah, or anything else, whether
>   through the speaker or on the TV/Roku screen. Pass:
>   - query: what they want (leave empty if they only named a channel + "latest video")
>   - channel: the channel name if mentioned, empty string if none
>   - latest_from_channel: true if they asked for that channel's newest/latest upload
>   - target: "speaker" if they want to just hear it, "tv" if they said TV/screen/Roku
>   If search_and_play returns a numbered list asking "which one?", just read that exact
>   reply back to the user as-is - don't add anything else, they'll answer with a number next."""
>
>
> def ask_gemini(user_text: str) -> str:
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, launch_app, search_and_play],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text

hacker@hacker:~ $ cat > wakeword.py << 'EOF'
> import time
> import sounddevice as sd
> import openwakeword
> from openwakeword.model import Model
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
> _pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
> _MODEL_PATH = f"{_pkg_dir}/resources/models/hey_jarvis_v0.1.onnx"
>
> owwModel = Model(wakeword_model_paths=[_MODEL_PATH])
>
>
> def listen_for_wakeword():
>     CHUNK = 1280
>     while True:
>         try:
>             with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                                  blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>                 print("Listening for wake word...")
>                 while True:
>                     audio, overflowed = stream.read(CHUNK)
>                     if overflowed:
>                         continue
>                     audio = audio.flatten()
>                     prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>                     for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                         if score > 0.5:
>                             print(f"Wake word detected: {mdl} ({score:.2f})")
>                             return
>         except Exception as e:
>             print(f"Mic stream error ({e}) - reconnecting in 2s...")
>             time.sleep(2)
>             continue
> EOF

> def parse_selection(text: str, max_choice: int):
>     text_low = text.lower()
>     m = re.search(r"\b(\d{1,2})\b", text_low)
>     if m:
>         n = int(m.group(1))
>         if 1 <= n <= max_choice:
>             return n
>     for word, n in _WORD_NUMBERS.items():
>         if re.search(rf"\b{word}\b", text_low) and n <= max_choice:
>             return n
>     return None

> SYSTEM_PROMPT = f"""You are {ASSISTANT_NAME}, a fast, friendly voice assistant like Alexa.
> The user speaks Hindi, Urdu, or English, sometimes mixed. ALWAYS reply in the SAME language
> the user just spoke in. If replying in Hindi, you MUST write in Devanagari script (e.g. नमस्ते),
> never Roman/Hinglish letters. If replying in Urdu, you MUST write in Urdu/Nastaliq script
> (e.g. سلام), never Roman letters. Keep answers SHORT and conversational (1-3 sentences) since
> your reply will be spoken out loud.
>
> Use tools whenever relevant, never guess live data:
> - get_current_time / get_weather for time and weather questions.
> - launch_app when the user wants to open an app on their TV/Roku with nothing specific to
>   play (e.g. "open Netflix on my TV").
> - search_and_play for ANY request to play a song, video, surah, or anything else, whether
>   through the speaker or on the TV/Roku screen. Pass:
>   - query: what they want (leave empty if they only named a channel + "latest video")
>   - channel: the channel name if mentioned, empty string if none
>   - latest_from_channel: true if they asked for that channel's newest/latest upload
>   - target: "speaker" if they want to just hear it, "tv" if they said TV/screen/Roku
>   If search_and_play returns a numbered list asking "which one?", just read that exact
>   reply back to the user as-is - don't add anything else, they'll answer with a number next."""
>
>
> def ask_gemini(user_text: str) -> str:
>     if not user_text:
>         return "Sorry, I didn't catch that."
>     response = client.models.generate_content(
>         model="gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
>         contents=user_text,
>         config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
>             system_instruction=SYSTEM_PROMPT,
>             tools=[get_current_time, get_weather, launch_app, search_and_play],
>         ),
>     )
>     return response.text
> EOF

 Model
from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX

_pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
_MODE> import time
> import sounddevice as sd
> import openwakeword
> from openwakeword.model import Model
> from config import MIC_DEVICE_INDEX
>
> _pkg_dir = openwakeword.__path__[0]
> _MODEL_PATH = f"{_pkg_dir}/resources/models/hey_jarvis_v0.1.onnx"
>
> owwModel = Model(wakeword_model_paths=[_MODEL_PATH])
>
>
> def listen_for_wakeword():
>     CHUNK = 1280
>     while True:
>         try:
>             with sd.InputStream(samplerate=16000, channels=1, dtype="int16",
>                                  blocksize=CHUNK, device=MIC_DEVICE_INDEX) as stream:
>                 print("Listening for wake word...")
>                 while True:
>                     audio, overflowed = stream.read(CHUNK)
>                     if overflowed:
>                         continue
>                     audio = audio.flatten()
>                     prediction = owwModel.predict(audio)
>                     for mdl, score in prediction.items():
>                         if score > 0.5:
>                             print(f"Wake word detected: {mdl} ({score:.2f})")
>                             return
>         except Exception as e:
>             print(f"Mic stream error ({e}) - reconnecting in 2s...")
>             time.sleep(2)
>             continue
> EOF
